She signed without question anything that Charlie put before her, but Hugh was constantly called in to explain all sorts of things. The position was difficult in the extreme, although Peggy tried to give Hugh good advice.

===Chinese===
那场大官司了结之后，库里昂（Kuryong）的生活恢复了旧日的节奏。玛丽·格兰特（Mary Grant）如今成了无可争议的主人，接过了管理大权，而休·戈登（Hugh Gordon）则被各种各样的借口留在了那里。

康西丁（Considine）和他的妻子在这一带住了一阵子，之后启程前往英国，他们和牧场主宅邸的人相处得极好——佩吉（Peggy）那次大胆的夺产图谋，看在她丈夫的面子上已经被原谅了。

玛丽似乎从让休来找她请示和商议这件事上，品味到了一种美妙的乐趣。查理·戈登（Charlie Gordon）放在她面前的东西她都二话不说签掉，却总把休叫来解释各种各样的事情。这个处境极其难堪，尽管佩吉竭力给休出些好主意。

===Original===
"Sure, the girl's fond of you, Mr. Hugh!" she said, "Why don't you ask her to marry you? See what a good thing it'd be? She's only waitin' to be asked."

"I'll manage my own affairs, thank you," said Hugh. "It isn't likely I'm going to ask her now, when I haven't got a penny." He was as miserable as a man could well be, and was on the point of leaving the station and going back to the buffalo camp in search of solitude, when an unexpected incident suddenly brought matters to a climax. A year had slipped by since William Grant's death, and the glorious Spring came round again; the river was bank-high with the melting of the mountain-snows, the English fruit-trees were all blossoming, and the willows a-bud. One day the mailman left a large handbill, anouncing the Spring race-meeting at Kiley's, a festival sacred, as a rule, to the Doyles and the Donohoes, at which no outsider had any earthly chance of winning a race.

===Chinese===
"当然了，那姑娘喜欢你，休先生！"她说，"你干嘛不跟她求婚呢？看看这多好的一桩事？她就等着人家开口呢。"

"我自己的事我自己管，谢谢你，"休说，"我现在身无分文，不可能去向她求婚。"他痛苦到了极点，正准备离开牧场，回到水牛营地去寻找清静，一个意想不到的插曲突然把事情推向了高潮。威廉·格兰特（William Grant）去世已一年，辉煌的春天又来了；山雪消融，河水涨到了岸边，英国果树繁花满枝，柳树也吐了新芽。一天，邮差留下一张大幅告示，宣布基利河（Kiley's River）的春季赛马会——这本来是多尔家（Doyles）和多诺霍家（Donohoes）的专属节日，外人根本没有赢的可能。

===Original===
In William Grant's time the handbill would have soon reached the fireplace; he did not countenance running station horses at the local meetings. Under the new owner things were different. Charlie Gordon was spoiling for a chance to run Revoke, a back-block purchase, against the locals, and suggested it in an offhand sort of way while reading the circular. Hugh opposed the notion altogether. His opposition apparently made Miss Grant determined to go on with the scheme, and she gave Charlie carte blanche in the matter.

When race-day arrived, there was quite a merry party at the homestead. Carew was making himself very attentive to Ellen Harriott, Mary was flirting very openly with Charlie Gordon, to Hugh's intense misery; and it was whispered about the station that the younger brother would be deposed in favour of the elder.

Hugh did not want to go to the races, but Mary asked him so directly that he had no option.

===Chinese===
在威廉·格兰特时代，这张告示早就进了壁炉——他不赞成拿牧场的马去参加地方赛马会。新主人当家就不一样了。查理·戈登正想找机会让他的"翻盘"（Revoke）——一匹从边远牧场买来的马——跟当地人比一比，读告示时随口提了出来。休完全反对这个主意。他的反对反而让格兰特小姐铁了心要搞，她给了查理全权委托。

赛马日到来，牧场主宅邸一片欢腾。卡鲁（Carew）对埃伦·哈里奥特（Ellen Harriott）大献殷勤，玛丽则毫不掩饰地跟查理·戈登打情骂俏，休痛苦万分；牧场里流传着悄悄话，说弟弟将被哥哥取代。

休不想去看赛马，但玛丽问得太直截了当了，他别无选择。

===Original===
It was a typical Australian Spring day. The sky was blue, the air was fresh, the breeze made great, long, rippling waves in the grass, and every soul in the place﻿—Mary in particular﻿—seemed determined to enjoy it to the utmost.

Revoke, the station champion, came in first in his race, and was promptly disqualified for short weight, but Mary didn't care.

"What is the use of worrying over it?" she said. "It doesn't really matter."

"I have been done," said the bushman. "Red Mick lent me the lead-cloth, and helped me saddle up, and I believe he took some lead out while we were saddling. It never dropped out. That I'm sure of."

"Oh, never mind, Mr. Gordon! Forget it! There's your brother, Hugh, thinks we ought not to have come, and now you are turning sulky. Why do all you Australian people amuse yourselves so sadly?"

===Chinese===
那是一个典型的澳大利亚春天。天空湛蓝，空气清新，微风在草地上掀起一道道长长的涟漪——这里每一个人，尤其是玛丽，都决心要尽情享受这一天。

"翻盘"，牧场的赛马冠军，在比赛中第一个冲线，却因负重不足被取消了资格，但玛丽并不在意。

"为这事烦恼有什么用？"她说，"真的不要紧。"

"我被耍了，"那个乡下人说，"红米克（Red Mick）借给我的铅布，还帮我备鞍，我相信他趁我们备鞍的时候抽掉了一些铅。铅从来就没掉下来过。这点我敢肯定。"

"哦，别在意了，戈登先生！忘了吧！你弟弟休觉得我们不该来，现在你又闹脾气了。你们澳大利亚人为什么玩得这么愁眉苦脸的？"

===Original===
"I don't know what you mean by sadly," said Charlie, huffed. "I think you ladies had better go home soon. Things are likely to be a bit lively later on. They have got a door off its hinges and laid on the ground, and a fiddler playing jigs, and the men and women are dancing each other down; it won't be long till there'll be a fight, and somebody will get stretched out."

Sure enough, they could see an excited crowd of people gathered round a fiddler, who was playing away for dear life, and the yells and whoops told them that partisanship was running high. All the young "bloods" of the ranges were there in their very best finery﻿—cabbage-tree hat (well-tilted back, and secured by a string under the nose), gaudy cotton shirt, and tweed trousers of loud pattern, secured round the waist by flaring red or green sashes. In this garb such as fancied themselves as dancers were taking their turns on the door.

===Chinese===
"我不知道你所谓的愁眉苦脸是什么意思，"查理气呼呼地说，"我觉得你们女士最好早点回去。等一会儿可能要出点热闹事。他们卸了一扇门板平放在地上，一个提琴手在拉吉格舞曲，男男女女跳得热火朝天；用不了多久就要打架了，有人得被放倒。"

果然，他们可以看到一群兴奋的人围着一个提琴手，那家伙拼了命地拉着琴，人们的叫喊声和尖叫声说明对立的情绪正在高涨。山区里所有的年轻"阔少"都来了，穿着他们最好的行头——甘蓝棕榈帽（帽檐高高翘起，用一根绳子系在鼻子底下），花哨的棉布衬衫，以及图案张扬的花呢裤子，腰间系着鲜红或翠绿的腰带。那些自认为会跳舞的人穿着这身行头轮番上阵。

===Original===
They began by ambling with a sort of strutting walk once or twice round the circumscribed platform; then, with head well back and eyes closed, dashed into the steps of the dance, each introducing varied steps and innovations of his own, which, if intricate and neatly executed, were greeted with great applause. So it happened that after Jerry the Swell, the recognised champion of the Doyles, had gone off with an extremely self-satisfied air, some adherents of young Red Mick, the opposition champion, took occasion to criticise Jerry's performance. "Darnce!" they said. "Jerry the Swell, darnce! Why, we've got an old poley cow would darnce him blind! Haven't we, Mick?"

"Yairs," said young Mick, with withering emphasis. "Darnce! He can't darnce. I'll run, darnce, jump, or fight any man in the district for two quid."

===Chinese===
他们先是在那块巴掌大的平台上趾高气扬地走上一两圈；然后头向后仰，眼睛闭上，一头扎进舞步，每个人都有自己独特的步法和花样——如果动作复杂又跳得利索，就会赢得满堂喝彩。就这样，多尔家公认的冠军"花花公子杰瑞"（Jerry the Swell）带着一副极其自满的神气跳完之后，对方冠军小红米克的几个拥护者趁机贬低杰瑞的表演。"跳舞！"他们说，"花花公子杰瑞，还跳舞！嘿，我们家有头老公牛都比他跳得好！对吧，米克？"

"是的，"小米克以令人泄气的强调语气说，"跳舞！他那叫跳舞！我跟他赌两镑——赛跑、跳舞、跳高、打架，随便哪样，这个区里随便谁我都奉陪。"

===Original===
Before the challenge could be accepted there was an unexpected interruption. Hugh had put the big trotting mare in the light trap for Miss Harriott and Mary to drive home. "Gentle Annie" was used to racing, and Hugh warned the girls to be careful in starting her, as she would probably be excited by the crowd, and then turned back to pack up the racing gear and start the four-in-hand with the children. As they were putting the racing saddle, bridles, and other gear into the vehicle, Charlie, who had been fuming ever since his defeat, caught sight of the missing lead-bag. He picked it up without a word, and with a fierce gleam in his eye, started over to the group of dancers, followed hurriedly by Carew. Just as young Mick was repeating his challenge to run, jump, dance, or fight anybody in the district, Gordon threw the lead-bag, weighing about six pounds, full in Red Mick's face.

"There's your lead, you thief!" he said. "Dance on that!"

===Chinese===
挑战还没人接呢，突然来了个意外的打断。休已经把那匹大快步母马套上了轻便马车，让哈里奥特小姐和玛丽坐车回家。"好脾气的安妮"（Gentle Annie）是匹赛马，休叮嘱姑娘们起步时小心点，因为它很可能会被人群惊到。然后他转身去收拾赛马用具，套上四马大车准备带孩子们走。正当他们把赛马鞍、笼头和其他器具往车上搬时，查理——自从输了比赛就一直在生闷气——看到了那个丢失的铅布袋。他一言不发地捡起它，眼中闪过凶狠的光芒，朝跳舞的人群走去，卡鲁急忙跟在后面。就在小米克重复着他的挑战——跟这个区里的任何人赛跑、跳高、跳舞或打架——的时候，戈登把那个重约六磅的铅布袋劈面砸在了红米克脸上。

"你的铅在这儿，你这个贼！"他说，"在这个上面跳吧！"

===Original===
Red Mick staggered back a pace or two, picked up an empty bottle from the ground, and made a dash at Gordon. The latter let out a vicious drive with his left that caught Mick under the ear and sent him down like a bullock. In a second the whole crowd surged together in one confused melée, everybody hitting at everybody amid a Babel of shouts and curses. The combat swayed out on to the racecourse, where half a dozen men fell over the ropes and pulled as many more down with them, and those that were down fought on the ground, while the others walked on them and fought over their heads. Carew, who was quite in his element, hit every head he saw, and knocked his knuckles to pieces on Black Andy Kelly's teeth. The fight he put up, and the terrific force of his hitting, are traditions among the mountain men to this day. Charlie Gordon was simply mad with the lust of fighting, and was locked in a death-grip with Red Mick; they swayed and struggled on the ground, while the crowd punched at them indiscriminately. In the middle of all this business, the two ladies and Alick, the eldest of the children, had started Gentle Annie for home, straight down the centre of the course. The big mare, hearing the yelling, and recognising that she was once more on a racetrack, suddenly caught hold of the bit, and came sweeping up the straight full-stretch, her great legs flying to and forth like pistons. Alick, who was sitting bodkin between the ladies, simply remarked, "Let her head go!" as she went thundering into the crowd, hurling Doyles and Donohoes into the air, trampling Kellys under foot﻿—and so out the other side, and away at a 2.30 gait for at least half a mile before the terrified girls could pull her up, and come back to see what damage had been done.

===Chinese===
红米克踉跄着后退了一两步，从地上捡起一个空瓶子，朝戈登扑去。戈登左手猛地一记重拳，打在米克耳朵底下，他像头公牛一样栽倒在地。一秒钟之内，整群人就卷入了一场混乱的混战，巴别塔般的咒骂声和叫喊声中，人人朝人人挥拳。打斗蔓延到了赛马场，六七个人绊倒了绳索，把同样多的人拽倒在地；倒地的人在地上继续打，站着的人踩着他们，从他们头顶上方交锋。卡鲁——他在这场面里如鱼得水——看到谁的头就打谁的头，一拳打在"黑人"安迪·凯利（Black Andy Kelly）的牙齿上，把指关节都打碎了。他那场架，还有他那力道惊人的拳击，至今仍是山民们传颂的传奇。查理·戈登简直杀红了眼，跟红米克死死扭在一起；两人在地上翻滚挣扎，人群毫无章法地朝他们乱揍。就在这片混乱当中，两位女士和最大的孩子亚历克（Alick）已经驾着"好脾气的安妮"朝家的方向走了，直直地沿着赛场中央驶去。大母马听到人群的喧闹，意识到自己又回到了赛马场上，突然咬紧嚼子，沿着直道全力冲刺起来，巨大的腿像活塞一样前后翻飞。亚历克挤坐在两位女士中间，只说了一句："放开它！"——大母马轰隆隆冲进人群，把多尔家和多诺霍家的人撞得人仰马翻，凯利家的人被踩在蹄下——然后冲到了另一边，以每英里两分半的速度狂奔了至少半英里，两个吓坏了的姑娘才把它拉住，掉头回来查看伤情。

===Original===
That ended the fight. The course was covered with wounded and disabled men. Some had been struck by the mare's hoofs; others had been run over by the wheels; and a great demand for whisky set in, under cover of which Gordon and Carew retired to the four-in-hand.

No one was seriously hurt, except "Omadhaun" Doyle, who had been struck on the head by the big mare's hoof. He lay very still, breathing stertorously, and Jerry the Swell took the trouble to come over to the four-in-hand, and inform them that he thought "Omadhaun" had got percussion of the brain, and that things looked very "omnibus" for him. However, as soon as he could swallow whisky he was pronounced out of the danger, and the Kuryong party was allowed to depart in peace for home, glad enough to get away. But the two girls were afraid to drive the big mare, as she was thoroughly roused after her dash in among the Doyles and Donohoes, and was inclined to show a lot of temper. A hurried consultation was held, with the result that Ellen Harriott and Alick were received into the four-in-hand, while Hugh was entrusted with the task of driving his employer home in the sulky.

===Chinese===
打架就此收场。赛场上满是伤兵残将。有人被母马的蹄子踢伤，有人被车轮碾过；威士忌的需求量激增，戈登和卡鲁趁乱退回了四马大车。

除了"傻瓜奥马多恩"（Omadhaun Doyle）之外，没有人受重伤——他被大母马的蹄子踢中了脑袋。他一动不动地躺着，呼吸沉重而有声。花花公子杰瑞费心走到四马大车旁，告诉他们他认为"傻瓜奥马多恩"得了脑震荡，情况很"完蛋"。不过，只要他能吞下威士忌，就算脱离危险了。于是库里昂一行人被允许平静地启程回家，大家巴不得赶紧离开。但两个姑娘不敢驾那匹大母马，因为它在多尔家和多诺霍家的人群中横冲直撞之后彻底兴奋起来了，脾气正大得很。一阵紧急磋商之后，埃伦·哈里奥特和亚历克被安排上了四马大车，而休则被委以重任——驾单座双轮马车送他的雇主回家。

===Original===
Now, a sulky is a vehicle built to accommodate two people only, and those two people have to sit fairly close together. For a few miles they spun along in silence, Hugh being well occupied with steadying the mare. From time to time he looked out of the corner of his eye at his companion; she looked steadily, almost stolidly, in front of her. Then she began to tap on the floor of the sulky with her foot. At last she turned on him.

"Well, we didn't win," she said. "I suppose you are glad."

"Why should I be glad, Miss Grant?"

"Oh! you said we oughn't to go and race among those people. And you were right. It served them just right that the mare ran over them. I hope that none of them are going to die."

"They wouldn't be much missed," said Hugh wearily. "They have started stealing the sheep again."

"Can't you catch them?" she said, with pretended asperity. "If you went out and hid in a fallen tree, don't you think you could catch them?"

===Chinese===
单座双轮马车是只能坐两个人的交通工具，而且两个人得坐得很近。有那么几英里路，他们一言不发地跑着，休专心地稳住母马。他时不时地用眼角余光瞥一眼同伴；她则目不斜视，几乎是木然地看着前方。然后她开始用脚轻轻敲打马车的地板。终于，她转过头来。

"好吧，我们没赢，"她说，"你一定很高兴。"

"我为什么该高兴呢，格兰特小姐？"

"哦！你说我们不该去跟那些人赛马。你是对的。母马踩了他们，活该。我希望他们不要死人。"

"少他们几个也没人会想念，"休疲惫地说，"他们又开始偷羊了。"

"你抓不到他们吗？"她故作严厉地说，"要是你出去躲在一棵倒下的树后面，你不觉得你能抓住他们吗？"

===Original===
Hugh looked at her to see if she were in earnest, but she looked straight in front again and said nothing, still keeping up the slight tapping of her foot. He flushed a little, and spoke very quietly.

"I think I'll have to resign from your employment, Miss Grant. I don't care about stopping any longer; and I will go out back and take up one of those twenty-thousand-acre leases in Queensland. You might put Poss or Binjie on in my place. They would be glad of a billet, and they might catch Red Mick for you."

"Do you really want to go?" she said, looking straight at him for the first time. "Why do you want to go?"

"Why?" he burst out. "Because I can't bear being with you and near you all day long, when I care for you, and you don't care for me. I can't eat, or sleep, or rest here now, and it's time I was away. You might give me a good character as a station-manager," he went on grimly, "even though I can't catch Red Mick for you. I'll get you to make out my cheque, and then I'll be off up North."

She was looking down now. The sun had gone, and the stars were peeping out, and in the dusk he could catch no glimpse of her face. There was silence for a few moments, then he went on talking, half to himself. "It's best for me, anyhow. It's time I made a start for myself. I couldn't stay on here as manager all my life."

Then she spoke, very low and quietly.

"You wouldn't care to stay on﻿—for anything else, then?"

"How do you mean for anything else, Miss Grant? You don't want me for anything except as manager, do you?"

"Well," she said, "you haven't asked me yet whether I do or not!"

===Chinese===
休看着她，想判断她是不是认真的，但她又直直地望着前方，不再说话，脚依旧轻轻点着地板。他脸微微一红，轻声说道。

"我想我得辞职了，格兰特小姐。我不想再待下去了；我要去内陆，在昆士兰（Queleigh）申请一块两万英亩的租地。你可以让波斯（Poss）或宾吉（Binjie）来接替我。他们会很乐意有这份差事，而且也许能帮你抓到红米克。"

"你真的想走吗？"她第一次直视着他，问道，"你为什么要走？"

"为什么？"他脱口而出，"因为我受不了整天跟您待在一起、离您这么近，我关心您，您却不关心我。我现在吃不下、睡不着、待不住，是时候离开了。您可以给我一份牧场管理人（station-manager）的好评语，"他冷冷地接着说，"尽管我抓不住红米克。我会请您开好我的支票，然后就北上去了。"

她此刻低下了头。太阳已经落山，星星探出了头，暮色中他看不清她的脸。沉默了片刻，他又接着说，半是自言自语："反正这样对我最好。是时候为我自己开始闯荡了。我不可能一辈子在这里当管理人。"

然后她开口了，声音又轻又低。

"那——你就不愿意留下来——为了别的什么？"

"您说'别的什么'是什么意思，格兰特小姐？除了当管理人，您不需要我做别的什么，对吗？"

"嗯，"她说，"您还从来没问过我需不需要呢！"

## Endnotes / 尾注

===Original===
1. One-eyed horse. ↩︎

===Chinese===
1. 独眼马。↩︎
